📖 遊戲說明書Game Manual

🧋 故事Story

台科大的夜晚，校園裡擺滿了夜市攤位，材料精靈丸丸們正忙著準備一場盛大的辦桌。沒想到調皮的石虎阿虎把工程材料的期中考題寫成「菜單卷」，塞進 20 個茄芷袋，藏在校園各個角落！One night at Taiwan Tech, the campus fills with night-market stalls while the little material spirits, the Marumaru, prepare a grand outdoor banquet. But Ahu, a mischievous Formosan leopard cat, wrote the Engineering Materials midterm questions onto "menu scrolls", stuffed them into 20 striped nylon bags and hid them all over campus!

總舖師黏土喵請你出馬：踩著藍白拖走遍校園、陪丸丸玩夜市遊戲、找回茄芷袋。全部找齊後辦桌開席，還能對統一發票看你中了什麼獎！Nendo, the head chef, needs your help: put on your slippers, explore campus, play night-market games with the Marumaru and recover the bags. Find them all to start the banquet and check which receipt-lottery prize you've won!

🗺️ 怎麼玩How to play

  1. 選擇模式Pick a mode
    📍 GPS 模式：拿著手機在校園實際走動（推薦）。🕹️ 遠端模式：不在校園時用搖桿或點地圖移動。📍 GPS mode: walk around campus with your phone (recommended). 🕹️ Remote mode: move with the joystick or by tapping the map when off campus.
  2. 找茄芷袋Find the bags
    地圖上的紅白藍茄芷袋就是藏菜單卷的地點。畫面下方會顯示「下一站」的方向與距離。Red-white-blue striped bags on the map mark the hidden scrolls. The chip at the bottom shows the direction and distance to the next one.
  3. 玩夜市遊戲Play a night-market game
    靠近茄芷袋時按「開始挑戰」，完成丸丸的小遊戲就能拿到菜單卷。When you're close, press "Play!" and finish the Marumaru's mini-game to win the scroll.
  4. 作答Answer
    菜單卷裡是 2025 期中考題。選擇題點選答案，計算題寫在框裡，最後標記「😀有把握／🤔不太確定／😵不會」。Each scroll holds 2025 midterm questions. Tap an option or write your working, then mark 😀 confident / 🤔 unsure / 😵 don't know.
  5. 收下茄芷袋Collect the bag
    按「收下茄芷袋」，丸丸就會跟著你走。找齊 20 個就能辦桌開席、對發票！Press "Collect the bag" and the Marumaru will follow you. Find all 20 for the banquet and the receipt lottery!

📌 這是考前複習，不計分、不提供答案。不確定的題目可以在 🎒 背包裡重看，也能匯出作答筆記帶去課堂討論。📌 This is ungraded review and no answers are given. Revisit uncertain questions in the 🎒 bag, or export your answers to discuss in class.

🎪 六種夜市遊戲Six night-market games

🍚大同電鍋Rice-cooker rescue電鍋蓋卡住了，連點 5 下把丸丸救出來。The lid is jammed; tap 5 times to free the Marumaru.
🌃招牌捉迷藏Neon-sign hide & seek開啟相機、轉動手機，在招牌之間找到丸丸（AR）。Open the camera and turn your phone to find the Marumaru among the signs (AR).
📷街景寫真Street snapshot依提示拍下植物、岩石、磨石子、紅磚或混凝土構造。Photograph a plant, rock, terrazzo, red brick or concrete element as prompted.
🧋搖手搖飲Shake the bubble tea15 秒內點擊或搖晃手機 25 下。Tap or shake 25 times within 15 seconds.
🎈夜市射氣球Balloon darts指針進入綠色區域時點擊，射破 3 顆氣球。Tap when the needle is in the green zone to pop 3 balloons.
🛵機車外送Scooter delivery騎機車再走 40 公尺（可以邊逛邊完成）。Ride 40 more meters (you can keep exploring meanwhile).

💡 小提醒Tips

  • ⚠️ 走路時請注意四周與來車，不要邊走邊盯著手機。Watch your surroundings and traffic; don't stare at your phone while walking.
  • 📍 請允許定位權限。大樓之間 GPS 可能有 10～30 公尺誤差，靠近後稍等一下即可。Allow location access. GPS can be off by 10–30 m between buildings; wait a moment once you are close.
  • 📷 招牌捉迷藏會要求相機與動作感應權限；不允許也能用拖曳方式玩。Neon-sign hide & seek asks for camera and motion access; you can still play by dragging.
  • 💾 遊戲會自動存檔。換手機前請到 ⚙️ 選單「匯出存檔檔案」。The game autosaves. Before switching phones, use ⚙️ Menu → Export save file.
  • ⛶ 建議按「全螢幕開始」，畫面會固定不會滑動或縮放。Use "Full screen & play" so the screen stays fixed without scrolling or zooming.
  • 🎵 背景音樂預設關閉，可在設定中開啟並選擇 20 首曲目。Music is off by default; turn it on in settings and pick from 20 tracks.

🎯 目標🎯 Goal
調皮的石虎阿虎把 2025 年工程材料期中考題塞進校園 20 個茄芷袋，由材料精靈「丸丸」看管。走到茄芷袋旁、完成夜市遊戲、作答後收下茄芷袋。找齊 20 個就能辦桌開席、對統一發票！Ahu the leopard cat stuffed the 2025 Engineering Materials midterm questions into 20 nylon bags on campus, guarded by the Marumaru spirits. Reach a bag, win its night-market game, answer the questions and collect it. Find all 20 for the banquet and the receipt lottery!

📍 GPS 模式📍 GPS mode
請允許瀏覽器使用定位。大樓之間 GPS 可能有 10～30 公尺誤差，靠近後稍等一下即可。Allow location access. GPS can be off by 10–30 m between buildings; wait a moment once you are close.

📝 作答📝 Answering
本遊戲為考前複習，不計分、不提供答案。請認真作答並標記把握程度，不確定的題目可在 🎒 背包中複習，或匯出作答筆記帶去課堂討論。This is ungraded exam review and no answers are given. Answer seriously and mark your confidence; review uncertain questions in the 🎒 bag, or export your answers to discuss in class.

⌨️ 操作⌨️ Controls
拖曳地圖可移動視角；遠端模式下點地圖可走過去。Drag to look around the map; in remote mode, tap the map to walk there.

考試附錄（第 10 頁）：常數與公式。Exam appendix (p. 10): constants & formulas.

摩斯MOS
水泥丸Cemmaru水泥Cement水泥遇水會水化，生成 C-S-H 膠體，把骨材牢牢黏在一起！Cement hydrates with water to form C-S-H gel that glues the aggregates together!
水滴丸Mizumaru拌合水Mixing water水灰比越低，強度通常越高；可是水太少就不好施工喔。A lower water–cement ratio usually means higher strength, but too little water hurts workability.
砂丸Sunamaru細骨材Fine aggregate細骨材會填進粗骨材之間的空隙，讓混凝土更密實。Fine aggregate fills the voids between coarse aggregate, making concrete denser.
石丸Ishimaru粗骨材Coarse aggregate粗骨材是混凝土的骨架，占的體積最多。Coarse aggregate is the skeleton of concrete and takes up the most volume.
飛灰丸Haimaru飛灰Fly ash飛灰是圓圓的小球，能改善工作性，還會慢慢產生卜作嵐反應。Fly ash particles are tiny spheres that improve workability and slowly react pozzolanically.
爐石丸Romaru爐石粉Slag (GGBFS)爐石粉來自煉鐵高爐，具有潛在水硬性。Ground slag comes from iron blast furnaces and is latently hydraulic.
矽灰丸Keimaru矽灰Silica fume矽灰超級細，能塞滿微小孔隙，讓混凝土更緻密。Silica fume is ultra-fine and packs tiny pores, making concrete denser.
黏土丸Nendomaru煅燒黏土Calcined clay煅燒黏土＋石灰石粉＝LC3，能大幅減少水泥的碳排放！Calcined clay + limestone = LC3, which greatly cuts the CO₂ of cement!
石灰丸Sekkaimaru石灰石粉Limestone powder石灰石粉提供成核位置，還能和鋁酸鹽相反應。Limestone powder provides nucleation sites and reacts with aluminate phases.
強塑丸Purumaru強塑劑Superplasticizer強塑劑讓水泥顆粒互相分散，少用水也能流動得很好。Superplasticizer disperses cement grains so the mix flows well with less water.
大同電鍋Rice-cooker rescue丸丸被關在大同電鍋裡了！電鍋蓋卡住，連續點擊 5 下把它打開。A Marumaru is stuck in a Tatung rice cooker! The lid is jammed. Tap it 5 times to pop it open.
招牌捉迷藏Neon-sign hide & seek丸丸躲在密密麻麻的招牌之間！舉起手機四處轉動（或拖曳畫面），找到牠並點擊 3 次。The Marumaru is hiding among the crowded shop signs! Turn your phone around (or drag the view), find it and tap it 3 times.
街景寫真Street snapshot依照下方提示拍一張照片，完成你的校園材料觀察。Take a photo as described below to complete your campus materials observation.
搖手搖飲Shake the bubble tea珍奶要搖才好喝！在 15 秒內點擊畫面（或搖晃手機）25 下！Bubble tea tastes best shaken! Tap the screen (or shake your phone) 25 times within 15 seconds!
夜市射氣球Balloon darts指針進入綠色區域時點擊射飛鏢，射破 3 顆氣球就過關！Tap to throw a dart when the needle is in the green zone. Pop 3 balloons to win!
機車外送Scooter delivery騎著機車再走 40 公尺，把菜單卷外送到辦桌現場！（可以先關閉視窗繼續逛，完成時會通知你。）Ride 40 more meters to deliver the menu scroll to the banquet! (You can close this and keep exploring; you'll be told when it's done.)
找一株植物或一片草，拍下它的樣子。想一想：風吹時，它的應力–應變曲線會長什麼樣子？Find a plant or some grass and photograph it. Think: what would its stress–strain curve look like in the wind?
拍下精誠石。它可能是哪一種岩石？耐得住火災的高溫嗎？Photograph the Jingcheng Stone. What kind of rock might it be? Could it survive the heat of a fire?
找一處磨石子地面、紅磚牆、空心磚或混凝土構造拍下來。磨石子是水泥＋石粒＋色粉研磨而成，你看得到石粒嗎？有裂縫或剝落嗎？Find a terrazzo floor, red-brick wall, hollow block or concrete element and photograph it. Terrazzo is cement + stone chips + pigment, ground smooth. Can you see the chips? Any cracks or spalling?
傍晚的台科大，校園裡悄悄冒出一整排夜市攤位，紅燈籠一盞一盞亮了起來⋯⋯Dusk at Taiwan Tech. A whole night market quietly pops up across campus, and the red lanterns light up one by one…
總舖師「黏土喵」和材料精靈「丸丸」們，正忙著準備今晚最盛大的辦桌！Head chef Nendo the cat and the little material spirits, the Marumaru, are busy preparing tonight's grand banquet!
可是調皮的石虎「阿虎」，把工程材料期中考題寫成「菜單卷」，一捲一捲塞進茄芷袋⋯⋯But Ahu, a mischievous Formosan leopard cat, wrote the Engineering Materials midterm questions onto menu scrolls and stuffed them into striped nylon bags…
⋯⋯再把 20 個茄芷袋藏到校園各處！每個袋子都有丸丸看守，陪牠們玩夜市遊戲才拿得回來。…then hid 20 bags all over campus! Each one is guarded by Marumaru, who only hand it back after a night-market game.
拿起手機打開地圖，踩著藍白拖出發！走到點位、完成任務、打開菜單卷複習考題。Grab your phone, open the map and set off in your slippers! Reach each spot, clear the game and open the menu scroll to review a question.
找齊 20 個茄芷袋，辦桌就開席，還能對統一發票，看看你中了哪一獎！Find all 20 bags to start the banquet, then check the receipt lottery to see which prize you've won!
準備好了嗎？今晚的夜市，就靠你了！Ready? Tonight's night market is counting on you!

喵～歡迎來到台科夜市！我是煅燒黏土貓「黏土喵」，今晚「丸丸辦桌」的總舖師。Meow~ Welcome to the Taiwan Tech Night Market! I'm Nendo, a calcined-clay cat and head chef of tonight's Marumaru banquet.

糟糕！調皮的石虎「阿虎」把期中考題菜單卷塞進 20 個茄芷袋，藏在校園各處，還交給材料精靈「丸丸」們看管！Oh no! Ahu, a mischievous Formosan leopard cat, stuffed the midterm question scrolls into 20 striped nylon bags, hid them all over campus and left the little material spirits, the Marumaru, in charge!

走到茄芷袋旁，陪丸丸玩一場夜市小遊戲，牠就會把菜單卷交給你，還會跟著你一起逛。Walk to a bag and play a night-market game with its Marumaru. It'll hand you the scroll and tag along with you.

找回全部 20 個茄芷袋，辦桌就能開席，還能對發票拿大獎！出發吧，喵！Find all 20 bags and the banquet can begin, plus you get to check your receipt lottery! Let's go, meow!

記得：走路時要注意四周和來車，不要邊走邊盯著手機喔！Remember: watch your surroundings and traffic, and don't stare at your phone while walking!

遠端模式可以用搖桿、方向鍵，或直接點地圖走過去。In remote mode, move with the joystick or arrow keys, or just tap the map to walk there.

看到發光的茄芷袋了嗎？靠近它，然後按「開始挑戰」！See the glowing bag? You're close enough. Press "Play!"

太棒了！考題不會自動批改，請認真作答；不確定的題目，之後可以在 🎒 背包裡複習。Great! Answers aren't auto-graded, so answer seriously. You can review uncertain ones later in the 🎒 bag.

已經找回 5 個茄芷袋了！累了就去超商吹冷氣、喝口水喵。5 bags found! Cool off in a convenience store and have some water if you're tired, meow.

一半了！辦桌的紅色圓桌已經擺好了喵！Halfway there! The red banquet tables are set up, meow!

只剩 5 個！總舖師的大鍋已經在滾了！Only 5 left! The chef's big pot is already boiling!

全部找齊！阿虎也乖乖道歉了。辦桌開席囉！All found! Ahu has apologised. Let the banquet begin!

恭喜完成！祝你期中考中特獎，喵～Congratulations! May your midterm hit the jackpot, meow~

特別獎Special Prize

中特別獎！你對每一組題目都很有把握，保持節奏、好好休息就好。Special Prize! You're confident on almost everything. Keep your rhythm and get some rest.

頭獎First Prize

中頭獎！大部分觀念都掌握了，把下面「建議再複習」的題目再看一次。First Prize! You've got most of it. Go over the questions listed below once more.

二獎Second Prize

中二獎！還有一些觀念不太確定，找同學一起討論，或回去翻翻課本吧。Second Prize! A few ideas are still shaky. Discuss them with classmates or revisit the textbook.

六獎Sixth Prize

中六獎～別灰心！考前把下面的題目逐一複習，下一期一定中大獎！Sixth Prize~ Don't lose heart! Review the questions below one by one and the jackpot is yours next time!
